-def calc_lot_size(equity, sl_points):

-    XAUUSD: 1 lot = 100 oz. 1 point ($0.01 move) = $1 P&L per lot.
-    So: dollars_per_point_per_lot = 1.0  (NOT 100 — that was the bug)
-    """

-    lots = risk_usd / (sl_points * dollars_per_point_per_lot)
-    lots = max(MIN_LOT, min(MAX_LOT, round(round(lots / si.volume_step) * si.volume_step, 2)))
-    risk_actual = sl_points * lots * dollars_per_point_per_lot

+    def profit_factor(self, trades: List[Dict]) -> float:
+        """
+        Gross Profit / Gross Loss.
+        > 1.5 = good, > 2.0 = excellent
+        """
+        closed = [t for t in trades if t.get('pnl_usd') is not None]
+        gross_profit = sum((t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) for t in closed if (t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) > 0)
+        gross_loss   = abs(sum((t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) for t in closed if (t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) < 0))
+        if gross_loss == 0:
+            return float('inf') if gross_profit > 0 else 0.0
+        return round(gross_profit / gross_loss, 3)
+
+    def expectancy(self, trades: List[Dict]) -> float:
+        """
+        Expected value per trade in USD.
+        Formula: (Win Rate × Avg Win) - (Loss Rate × Avg Loss)
+        Positive = profitable system.
+        """
+        closed = [t for t in trades if t.get('pnl_usd') is not None]
+        if not closed:
+            return 0.0
+
+        wins   = [t for t in closed if (t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) > 0]
+        losses = [t for t in closed if (t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) < 0]
+
+        wr   = len(wins) / len(closed)
+        lr   = 1 - wr
+        avg_win  = sum(t.get('pnl_usd', 0) for t in wins)  / len(wins)  if wins   else 0
+        avg_loss = abs(sum(t.get('pnl_usd', 0) for t in losses)) / len(losses) if losses else 0
+
+        return round((wr * avg_win) - (lr * avg_loss), 2)
+
+    def max_drawdown(self, equity_curve: List[Dict]) -> Tuple[float, float]:
+        """
+        Maximum drawdown from equity curve.
+        Returns (max_dd_usd, max_dd_pct).
+        """
+        if not equity_curve:
+            return 0.0, 0.0
+
+        equities = [float(e.get('equity', 0)) for e in equity_curve]
+        peak     = equities[0]
+        max_dd   = 0.0
+        max_peak = peak
+
+        for eq in equities:
+            if eq > peak:
+                peak = eq
+            dd = peak - eq
+            if dd > max_dd:
+                max_dd = dd
+            if eq > max_peak:
+                max_peak = eq
+
+        max_dd_pct = (max_dd / max_peak * 100) if max_peak > 0 else 0
+        return round(max_dd, 2), round(max_dd_pct, 3)
+
+    def sharpe_ratio(self, trades: List[Dict],
+                     risk_free_rate_daily: float = 0.0) -> float:
+        """
+        Annualized Sharpe Ratio.
+        Uses daily P&L returns, annualized by √252.
+        """
+        closed = [t for t in trades if t.get('pnl_usd') is not None]
+        if len(closed) < 5:
+            return 0.0
+
+        # Group by day
+        daily_pnl: Dict[str, float] = {}
+        for t in closed:
+            ts = t.get('timestamp_close', '')
+            day = ts[:10] if ts else 'unknown'
+            daily_pnl[day] = daily_pnl.get(day, 0) + (t.get('pnl_usd') or 0)
 
-    def _group_performance(self, trades: List[Dict], key: str) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
-        groups: Dict[str, List[float]] = defaultdict(list)
+        returns = list(daily_pnl.values())
+        if len(returns) < 2:
+            return 0.0
+
+        mean_return = sum(returns) / len(returns)
+        variance    = sum((r - mean_return) ** 2 for r in returns) / (len(returns) - 1)
+        std_dev     = math.sqrt(variance) if variance > 0 else 1e-9
+
+        sharpe = (mean_return - risk_free_rate_daily) / std_dev * math.sqrt(252)
+        return round(sharpe, 3)
+
+    def avg_trade_duration(self, trades: List[Dict]) -> float:
+        """Average trade duration in minutes."""
+        closed = [t for t in trades
+                  if t.get('duration_minutes') and t.get('timestamp_close')]
+        if not closed:
+            return 0.0
+        total = sum(t.get('duration_minutes', 0) for t in closed)
+        return round(total / len(closed), 1)
+
+    def calmar_ratio(self, trades: List[Dict],
+                     equity_curve: List[Dict]) -> float:
+        """
+        Annualized Return / Max Drawdown.
+        Higher = better risk-adjusted return.
+        """
+        if not trades or not equity_curve:
+            return 0.0
+        total_pnl = sum((t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) for t in trades
+                        if t.get('pnl_usd') is not None)
+        _, max_dd_pct = self.max_drawdown(equity_curve)
+        if max_dd_pct == 0:
+            return float('inf') if total_pnl > 0 else 0.0
+        return round(total_pnl / (max_dd_pct / 100), 2)
+
+    def consecutive_losses(self, trades: List[Dict]) -> int:
+        """Maximum consecutive losing streak."""
+        closed = sorted(
+            [t for t in trades if t.get('pnl_usd') is not None and t.get('timestamp_close')],
+            key=lambda x: x.get('timestamp_close', '')
+        )
+        max_streak = streak = 0
+        for t in closed:
+            if (t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) < 0:
+                streak += 1
+                max_streak = max(max_streak, streak)
+            else:
+                streak = 0
+        return max_streak
+
+    def recovery_factor(self, trades: List[Dict],
+                        equity_curve: List[Dict]) -> float:
+        """Net profit / Max drawdown — measures recovery ability."""
+        net_profit = sum((t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) for t in trades
+                         if t.get('pnl_usd') is not None)
+        max_dd, _ = self.max_drawdown(equity_curve)
+        if max_dd == 0:
+            return float('inf') if net_profit > 0 else 0.0
+        return round(net_profit / max_dd, 3)
+

+    # ─── Grade Analytics ─────────────────────────────────
+
+    def grade_performance(self, trades: List[Dict]) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
+        """
+        Break down performance by trade quality grade (A+, A, B, C).
+        Returns per-grade metrics:
+          - Win Rate
+          - Profit Factor
+          - Expectancy
+          - Average R-Multiple
+          - Expected Value
+          - Total P&L
+          - Average Drawdown
+        """
+        grades: Dict[str, List] = {}
+        for t in trades:
+            if t.get('pnl_usd') is None:
                 continue
-        return durations
+            g = t.get('quality_grade', 'Unknown') or 'Unknown'
+            grades.setdefault(g, []).append(t)
+
+        result = {}
+        for g, g_trades in grades.items():
+            stats = self._compute_group_stats(g_trades)
+
+            # Compute average R-multiple
+            r_multiples = []
+            for t in g_trades:
+                risk_usd = t.get('risk_usd')
+                pnl = t.get('pnl_usd', 0) or 0
+                if risk_usd and risk_usd > 0:
+                    r_multiples.append(pnl / risk_usd)
+
+            avg_r = sum(r_multiples) / len(r_multiples) if r_multiples else 0.0
+            expected_value = stats.get('expectancy', 0.0)
+
+            stats['avg_r_multiple'] = round(avg_r, 3)
+            stats['expected_value'] = round(expected_value, 2)
+            stats['grade'] = g
+            result[g] = stats
+
+        return result

+    # ─── Full Report ───────────────────────────────────────
+
+    def full_performance_report(self, trades: List[Dict],
+                                equity_curve: List[Dict],
+                                period: str = "All Time") -> Dict:
+        """
+        Master report combining all metrics.
+        Returns a comprehensive analytics dict.
+        """
+        closed = [t for t in trades if t.get('pnl_usd') is not None]
+        wins   = [t for t in closed if (t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) > 0]
+        losses = [t for t in closed if (t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) < 0]
+
+        total_pnl  = sum((t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) for t in closed)
+        avg_win    = sum(t.get('pnl_usd', 0) for t in wins) / len(wins) if wins else 0
+        avg_loss   = abs(sum(t.get('pnl_usd', 0) for t in losses)) / len(losses) if losses else 0
+        max_dd_usd, max_dd_pct = self.max_drawdown(equity_curve)
+
+        # Best/worst pairs
+        pair_stats = self.pair_performance(trades)
+        best_pair  = max(pair_stats, key=lambda k: pair_stats[k]['total_pnl'], default="")
+        worst_pair = min(pair_stats, key=lambda k: pair_stats[k]['total_pnl'], default="")
+
+        # Best session
+        sess_stats  = self.session_performance(trades)
+        best_session = max(sess_stats, key=lambda k: sess_stats[k]['win_rate'], default="")
+
+        # Grade analytics
+        grade_stats = self.grade_performance(trades)
+
+        report = {
+            "period":               period,
+            "generated_at":         datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
+
+            # Volume
+            "total_trades":         len(closed),
+            "winning_trades":       len(wins),
+            "losing_trades":        len(losses),
+            "open_trades":          len(trades) - len(closed),
+
+            # Core metrics
+            "win_rate":             self.win_rate(trades),
+            "profit_factor":        self.profit_factor(trades),
+            "expectancy":           self.expectancy(trades),
+            "sharpe_ratio":         self.sharpe_ratio(trades),
+
+            # P&L
+            "total_pnl":            round(total_pnl, 2),
+            "avg_pnl":              round(total_pnl / len(closed), 2) if closed else 0,
+            "avg_win":              round(avg_win, 2),
+            "avg_loss":             round(avg_loss, 2),
+            "largest_win":          round(max((t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) for t in closed), 2) if closed else 0,
+            "largest_loss":         round(min((t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) for t in closed), 2) if closed else 0,
+
+            # Risk metrics
+            "max_drawdown_usd":     max_dd_usd,
+            "max_drawdown_pct":     max_dd_pct,
+            "max_consecutive_losses": self.consecutive_losses(trades),
+            "recovery_factor":      self.recovery_factor(trades, equity_curve),
+            "calmar_ratio":         self.calmar_ratio(trades, equity_curve),
+
+            # Duration
+            "avg_duration_minutes": self.avg_trade_duration(trades),
+
+            # Breakdowns
+            "best_pair":            best_pair,
+            "worst_pair":           worst_pair,
+            "best_session":         best_session,
+            "session_stats":        sess_stats,
+            "pair_stats":           pair_stats,
+            "strategy_stats":       self.strategy_performance(trades),
+            "regime_stats":         self.regime_performance(trades),
+            "grade_stats":          grade_stats,
+        }
+        return report

+    # ─── Rejection Analysis ────────────────────────────────

+    def analyze_rejections(self, rejections: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
+        """
+        Analyze patterns in rejected trades to guide parameter tuning.
+        Ported from trading_bot2's get_rejection_analysis().
+        """
+        if not rejections:
+            return {"message": "No rejections recorded yet"}
+
+        total = len(rejections)
+        struct_fails = sum(1 for r in rejections if not r.get('structure_pass'))
+        liq_fails    = sum(1 for r in rejections if not r.get('liquidity_pass'))
+        mom_fails    = sum(1 for r in rejections if not r.get('momentum_pass'))
+        vol_fails    = sum(1 for r in rejections if not r.get('volume_pass'))
+
+        # Most common rejection reason
+        reasons: Dict[str, int] = {}
+        for r in rejections:
+            reason = r.get('reason_for_rejection', 'Unknown')
+            # Shorten to category
+            key = reason.split(':')[0].strip() if ':' in reason else reason[:40]
+            reasons[key] = reasons.get(key, 0) + 1
+
+        top_reasons = sorted(reasons.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:5]
+
+        # Most common regime for rejections
+        regimes: Dict[str, int] = {}
+        for r in rejections:
+            reg = r.get('regime', 'unknown') or 'unknown'
+            regimes[reg] = regimes.get(reg, 0) + 1
+
+        worst_regime = max(regimes, key=regimes.get) if regimes else "unknown"
+
+        # Recommendation
+        fails = {
+            "Structure": struct_fails, "Liquidity": liq_fails,
+            "Momentum":  mom_fails,    "Volume":    vol_fails
+        }
+        worst_filter = max(fails, key=fails.get)
+        worst_pct    = fails[worst_filter] / total * 100 if total > 0 else 0
+
+        if worst_pct > 50:
+            recommendation = (f"Focus on {worst_filter}: {worst_pct:.0f}% of rejections fail this filter. "
+                              f"Consider loosening {worst_filter.lower()} parameters.")
         else:
-            # Build synthetic equity from cumulative PnL
-            equities = []
-            cum = 0
-            for p in pnls:
-                cum += p
-                equities.append(cum + 10000)  # assume 10k base

-        max_dd = 0

-            if eq > peak:
-                peak = eq

-                dd = (peak - eq) / peak
-                max_dd = max(max_dd, dd)

+    # ─── Helper ────────────────────────────────────────────

+    def _compute_group_stats(self, trades: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
+        """Compute standard stats for a group of trades."""
+        if not trades:
+            return {}
+        closed = [t for t in trades if t.get('pnl_usd') is not None]
+        wins   = [t for t in closed if (t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) > 0]
+        total_pnl = sum((t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) for t in closed)
+        avg_win  = sum(t.get('pnl_usd', 0) for t in wins) / len(wins) if wins else 0
+        avg_loss_list = [t for t in closed if (t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) < 0]
+        avg_loss = abs(sum(t.get('pnl_usd', 0) for t in avg_loss_list)) / len(avg_loss_list) if avg_loss_list else 0
+        wr       = len(wins) / len(closed) * 100 if closed else 0
+        pf       = self.profit_factor(closed)
+        exp      = self.expectancy(closed)
+        avg_rr   = sum((t.get('risk_reward_ratio') or 0) for t in closed) / len(closed) if closed else 0

+
+    def daily(self) -> Dict:
+        """
+        Return daily statistics for today's trades.
+        """
+        from zohra.db.schema import db
+        # Get trades closed in the last 1 day
+        closed_trades = db.get_closed_trades(limit=100, days=1)
+        # We can calculate daily stats
+        wins = [t for t in closed_trades if (t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) > 0]
+        losses = [t for t in closed_trades if (t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) < 0]
+        total_pnl = sum((t.get('pnl_usd') or 0) for t in closed_trades)
+        wr = (len(wins) / len(closed_trades) * 100) if closed_trades else 0.0
+        
+        return {
+            "total_trades": len(closed_trades),
+            "winning_trades": len(wins),
+            "losing_trades": len(losses),
+            "win_rate_pct": round(wr, 2),
+            "net_pnl": round(total_pnl, 2),
+            "total_pnl": round(total_pnl, 2)
         }

-        return self.full_report(since=since)

-        return self.full_report(since=since)

+    def __getattr__(self, name):
+        import sys
+        module = sys.modules[__name__]
+        if hasattr(module, name):
+            return getattr(module, name)
+        raise AttributeError(f"'{self.__class__.__name__}' object has no attribute '{name}'")
+
+    def __setattr__(self, name, value):
+        super().__setattr__(name, value)
+        import sys
+        module = sys.modules.get(__name__)
+        if module and hasattr(module, name):
+            setattr(module, name, value)

+            f"⚖️ Profit Factor: <b>{pf:.2f}</b>\n"
+            f"💰 Expectancy: <b>${exp:.2f}/trade</b>\n"
+            f"📉 Max DD: <b>{dd_pct:.2f}%</b>\n"
+            f"📊 Sharpe: <b>{sharpe:.2f}</b>\n\n"
+            f"🔢 Total Trades: {trades}\n"
+            f"🏆 Best Pair: {best_p}\n"
+            f"⚠️ Worst Pair: {worst_p}\n\n"
+            f"⏰ {datetime.utcnow().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')} UTC"
         )
+        return msg

+    # ─── Chart Generators ──────────────────────────────────

+    def _generate_equity_chart(self, equity_curve: List[Dict],
+                                filename: str, title: str = "Equity") -> Optional[str]:
+        if not MATPLOTLIB_AVAILABLE or not equity_curve:
+            return None
+
+        path = os.path.join(REPORTS_DIR, filename)
+        try:
+            times    = [e.get('timestamp', '')[:19] for e in equity_curve]
+            equities = [float(e.get('equity', 0)) for e in equity_curve]
+
+            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14, 5))
+            color = '#00d4aa'
+            ax.plot(range(len(equities)), equities, color=color, linewidth=2)
+            ax.fill_between(range(len(equities)), equities,
+                            min(equities), alpha=0.15, color=color)
+            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=14, pad=12, color='white')

+            plt.close(fig)

+            logger.error(f"Equity chart error: {e}")

+    def _generate_session_chart(self, trades: List[Dict],
+                                 filename: str, title: str = "Session Performance") -> Optional[str]:
+        if not MATPLOTLIB_AVAILABLE:
+            return None
+
+        path = os.path.join(REPORTS_DIR, filename)
+        try:
+            sess_pnl: Dict[str, float] = {}
+            sess_wr:  Dict[str, List]  = {}
+            for t in trades:
+                sess = t.get('session', 'Unknown') or 'Unknown'
+                pnl  = t.get('pnl_usd') or 0
+                sess_pnl[sess] = sess_pnl.get(sess, 0) + pnl
+                sess_wr.setdefault(sess, []).append(1 if pnl > 0 else 0)
+
+            if not sess_pnl:
+                return None
+
+            sessions = list(sess_pnl.keys())
+            pnls     = list(sess_pnl.values())
+            wrs      = [sum(sess_wr[s]) / len(sess_wr[s]) * 100 for s in sessions]
+            colors   = ['#00d4aa' if p >= 0 else '#ff6b6b' for p in pnls]
+
+            fig, (ax1, ax2) = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(14, 5))
+
+            ax1.bar(sessions, pnls, color=colors)
+            ax1.set_title("P&L by Session", color='white')
+            ax1.set_ylabel("P&L ($)", color='white')
+            ax1.set_facecolor('#1a1a2e')
+            ax1.tick_params(colors='gray')
+            ax1.grid(True, axis='y', alpha=0.15, color='gray')
+            ax1.axhline(0, color='gray', linewidth=0.8)
+
+            ax2.bar(sessions, wrs, color='#4ecdc4')
+            ax2.axhline(50, color='gray', linewidth=0.8, linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)
+            ax2.set_title("Win Rate by Session", color='white')
+            ax2.set_ylabel("Win Rate (%)", color='white')
+            ax2.set_ylim(0, 100)
+            ax2.set_facecolor('#1a1a2e')
+            ax2.tick_params(colors='gray')
+            ax2.grid(True, axis='y', alpha=0.15, color='gray')
+
+            fig.patch.set_facecolor('#16213e')
+            fig.suptitle(title, fontsize=13, color='white')
+            plt.tight_layout()
+            plt.savefig(path, dpi=120, bbox_inches='tight', facecolor=fig.get_facecolor())
+            plt.close(fig)
+            return path
+        except Exception as e:
+            logger.error(f"Session chart error: {e}")
+            return None
+
+    def _generate_drawdown_chart(self, trades: List[Dict],
+                                  filename: str, title: str = "Drawdown") -> Optional[str]:
+        if not MATPLOTLIB_AVAILABLE:
+            return None
+
+        path = os.path.join(REPORTS_DIR, filename)
+        try:
+            # Build equity series from cumulative PnL
+            pnls  = [t.get('pnl_usd') or 0 for t in trades if t.get('pnl_usd') is not None]
+            equity = []
+            running = 0
+            for p in pnls:
+                running += p
+                equity.append(running)
+
+            if not equity:
+                return None
+
+            # Drawdown series
+            peak = equity[0]
+            dd   = []
+            for eq in equity:
+                if eq > peak: peak = eq
+                dd.append(eq - peak)
+
+            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14, 4))
+            ax.fill_between(range(len(dd)), dd, 0, alpha=0.7, color='#ff6b6b')
+            ax.plot(range(len(dd)), dd, color='#ff4444', linewidth=1)
+            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, color='white')
+            ax.set_ylabel("Drawdown ($)", color='white')
+            ax.set_facecolor('#1a1a2e')
+            fig.patch.set_facecolor('#16213e')
+            ax.tick_params(colors='gray')
+            ax.grid(True, alpha=0.15, color='gray')
+            plt.tight_layout()
+            plt.savefig(path, dpi=120, bbox_inches='tight', facecolor=fig.get_facecolor())
+            plt.close(fig)
+            return path
+        except Exception as e:
+            logger.error(f"Drawdown chart error: {e}")
+            return None
+
+    def _generate_pnl_histogram(self, trades: List[Dict],
+                                 filename: str, title: str = "P&L Distribution") -> Optional[str]:
+        if not MATPLOTLIB_AVAILABLE:
+            return None
+
+        path = os.path.join(REPORTS_DIR, filename)
+        try:
+            pnls = [t.get('pnl_usd') or 0 for t in trades if t.get('pnl_usd') is not None]
+            if not pnls:
+                return None
+
+            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 5))
+            ax.hist([p for p in pnls if p > 0], bins=20, color='#00d4aa', alpha=0.7, label='Wins')
+            ax.hist([p for p in pnls if p < 0], bins=20, color='#ff6b6b', alpha=0.7, label='Losses')
+            ax.axvline(0, color='white', linewidth=1, linestyle='--')
+            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, color='white')
+            ax.set_xlabel("P&L ($)", color='white')
+            ax.set_ylabel("Count", color='white')
+            ax.legend(facecolor='#1a1a2e', edgecolor='gray', labelcolor='white')
+            ax.set_facecolor('#1a1a2e')
+            fig.patch.set_facecolor('#16213e')
+            ax.tick_params(colors='gray')
+            ax.grid(True, alpha=0.15, color='gray')
+            plt.tight_layout()
+            plt.savefig(path, dpi=120, bbox_inches='tight', facecolor=fig.get_facecolor())
+            plt.close(fig)
+            return path
+        except Exception as e:
+            logger.error(f"P&L histogram error: {e}")
+            return None

+    def _generate_score_distribution(self, trades: List[Dict],
+                                      filename: str, title: str = "Score Distribution") -> Optional[str]:
+        if not MATPLOTLIB_AVAILABLE:
+            return None

+        path = os.path.join(REPORTS_DIR, filename)

+            plt.close(fig)

-def find_swings(df, lookback=50, pivot=3):
-    """
-    Returns swing highs and lows using n-bar pivot logic.
-    A swing high: high[i] is highest of high[i-n:i+n+1]
-    A swing low : low[i]  is lowest  of  low[i-n:i+n+1]
-    """

-    subset = df.iloc[-(lookback+pivot*2):-1].reset_index(drop=True)
-    n = pivot
-    for i in range(n, len(subset)-n):
-        window_h = subset['high'].iloc[i-n:i+n+1]
-        window_l = subset['low'].iloc[i-n:i+n+1]
-        if subset['high'].iloc[i] == window_h.max():
-            highs.append({'price': subset['high'].iloc[i],
-                          'time':  subset['time'].iloc[i],
-                          'idx':   i})
-        if subset['low'].iloc[i] == window_l.min():
-            lows.append({'price': subset['low'].iloc[i],
-                         'time':  subset['time'].iloc[i],
-                         'idx':   i})
-    return highs, lows

-def detect_structure(df_h4, df_h1):

-    Returns: bias ('BULL'|'BEAR'|'NEUTRAL'), bos, choch details.

-

-                result['choch'] = 'BULL_CHOCH'

-            # Uptrend — watch for bearish CHOCH

-                result['choch'] = 'BEAR_CHOCH'

-        result['bias'] = 'BEAR'
-    else:
-        result['bias'] = 'NEUTRAL'  # conflicting TFs

-    return result

-    """

-    Bullish FVG: gap between candle[i-1].high and candle[i+1].low

-    Returns list of FVG dicts, sorted freshest first.
-    """

-
-    for i in range(1, n_sub - 1):

-        c_mid  = subset.iloc[i]

-
-        # Bullish FVG

-            size = c_next['low'] - c_prev['high']

-                        "type":      "BULL",
-                        "top":       top,
-                        "bottom":    bottom,
-                        "mid":       mid,
-                        "size":      size,
-                        "age":       max_age_candles - i,
-                        "candle_time": c_mid['time'],
-                        "filled":    filled,
-                    })
-
-        # Bearish FVG

-            size = c_prev['low'] - c_next['high']

-                        "bottom":    bottom,
-                        "mid":       mid,
-                        "size":      size,
-                        "age":       max_age_candles - i,
-                        "candle_time": c_mid['time'],
-                        "filled":    filled,
-                    })
-
-    # Sort: freshest first, then largest

-def print_stats():
-    try:
-        df_j = pd.read_csv(JOURNAL)
-        df_closed = df_j[df_j['exit_reason'] != 'OPEN']

-        avg_rr  = df_closed['r_multiple'].astype(float).mean()

-        win_pnl = df_closed[df_closed['pnl_usd'].astype(float) > 0]['pnl_usd'].astype(float).sum()
